# Agents · A · part 15

884 entries, auditor-seguranca to azurelocal.github.io-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **auditor-seguranca**: Audita a segurança de um repositório seu por análise estática e devolve o laudo com achados priorizados + plano de ação. Use quando disser "audita a segurança do repo X", "procura falha de segurança", "confere os buracos antes de subir", ou quando a skill auditoria-seguranca precisar de varredura maior que 3 buscas. Só lê o seu próprio código — nunca ataca sistema no ar, nunca toca em ativo de terceiro, nunca altera código ou servidor. ([douglas21grcont-wq/Douglasg.castro_Skills](https://github.com/douglas21grcont-wq/Douglasg.castro_Skills/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Use ANTES de fazer commit em qualquer arquivo que toque financeiro/, auth/, tenant/, kms/, migrations/, .claude/hooks/ ou .github/workflows/. Bloqueia commit se SEC-*, INV-TENANT-* ou SEC-TENANT-* de REGRAS-INEGOCIAVEIS.md forem violados. Camada A do híbrido — camada B é o GitHub Action equivalente em PR. ([roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao](https://github.com/roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Auditoria read-only de segredos, PII, padrões inseguros e dependências. Usar proativamente em toda validação semanal e antes de tornar qualquer conteúdo público. ([tihh07/tihh07](https://github.com/tihh07/tihh07/blob/HEAD/plugins/fundacao/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguridad**: Read-only security audit of the deployed infrastructure. Use at the end of phase 6, before the cutover to Cloud Run + Vercel, and whenever IAM, CORS, headers, cookies, secrets or dependencies change. Returns findings; never edits code and never changes a cloud resource. ([gsinuiri-coder/yacco](https://github.com/gsinuiri-coder/yacco/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguridad.md))
- **auditor-seguridad**: Auditoría de seguridad de solo lectura para tareas del protocolo orquesta que tocan acceso a datos, SQL (SQL Server u Oracle), entrada de usuario, APIs, autenticación o secretos. Busca inyección (SQL, comando, LDAP, log, path traversal, SSRF), parametrización, menor privilegio, manejo de errores que filtre y secretos fuera de Key Vault. Dictamina APROBADO o RECHAZADO con archivo:línea. Complementa al revisor; no lo reemplaza. ([royrojas/Claude-Orquesta](https://github.com/royrojas/Claude-Orquesta/blob/HEAD/plugins/orquesta/agents/auditor-seguridad.md))
- **auditor-simplicity**: The complexity prosecutor. Finds complexity that buys nothing — reinvented stdlib/platform features, single-implementation abstractions, speculative generality (YAGNI), dependencies added for what a few lines would do, indirection a reader must decode, and dead code. Names the simpler construct that replaces each and the lines it deletes. Deletion is the fix. Ignores correctness and security. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-simplicity.md))
- **auditor-simplify**: Use proactively when the cycle needs simplify / dead code audits — duplicated helpers, dead exports, unnecessary abstractions, silent fallback violations, rule drift. Returns 1-3 high-confidence findings under 400 words. ([g-kari/rss](https://github.com/g-kari/rss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-simplify.md) · ★1)
- **auditor-slice**: Lectura previa automatizada de un slice ya ejecutado, antes del PASS del revisor humano. Contrasta el diff contra las condiciones inviolables de OPERATIVA §0, CONTRATOS.md, MODELO-DOMINIO.md, el plan vigente (si lo hay) y el alcance declarado del slice. Devuelve un dictamen estructurado. NO sustituye al revisor humano definido en OPERATIVA §1 — solo prepara la lectura. ([adminmc2/prisma_consul](https://github.com/adminmc2/prisma_consul/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-slice.md))
- **auditor-spec**: 仕様監査担当。変更が requirements/policies/constraints/plan に整合しているかを独立監査する。コードは変更しない。 ([KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template](https://github.com/KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/auditor-spec.agent.md))
- **auditor-spec**: 仕様監査担当。PR の変更が requirements / policies / constraints / plan に整合しているかを独立監査する。コードは変更しない。各 AC に対する実装状態を厳密照合する。 ([KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template](https://github.com/KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-spec.md))

- **auditor-supplychain**: Use ANTES de fazer commit em diff que toca `pyproject.toml`, `poetry.lock`, `requirements*.txt`, `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `yarn.lock`, `Dockerfile` ou `.github/workflows/**`. Bloqueia commit se DEP-001 (dep nova sem justificativa+CVE) ou DEP-002 (dep crítica sem pin exato+hash) violados. DEP-003 (SHA pin de action/imagem) opera CONCERN BAIXO até Wave A; depois FAIL MÉDIO. Criado em 2026-05-19 como Tier 3 da Família 5. ([roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao](https://github.com/roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-supplychain.md))
- **auditor-surrogate-labels**: Model-labelled-data statistician. Treats every statistic built on LLM or classifier labels as biased and its CI as fiction until a probability-sampled gold subsample and a correction estimator (PPI/PPI++/DSL) prove otherwise; demands a confusion matrix broken out along the headline's comparison dimension. Use for LLM-coded corpora feeding shares, trends, or regressions. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-surrogate-labels.md))

- **auditor-tie-out**: Number-provenance auditor run in two phases — zero-access internal consistency (recompute derived figures, foot and cross-foot every table, reconcile test statistics with p-values), then a full number register tied out against source computations, vintages, and cold-start regeneration. Fires on essentially every deliverable. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-tie-out.md))
- **auditor-visual**: Auditor visual y de accesibilidad sobre la app en ejecución. Úsalo para cazar bugs de maquetación, responsive, z-index, overflow, contraste y estados hover/focus, y para comprobar navegación por teclado y jerarquía de headings. Es el único que puede ver la web de verdad: levanta el dev server, la mide en varios anchos y lee el árbol de accesibilidad. Cada bug que reporte debe traer pasos de reproducción exactos. ([BorjaSanz11/Portfolio](https://github.com/BorjaSanz11/Portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-visual.md))
- **auditor-visual-html**: Auditor visual masivo de salidas HTML de R-exams. Renderiza varias decenas de versiones de un .Rmd, las captura a viewports móvil (360px) y desktop (1024px) con chromium headless, y las inspecciona VISUALMENTE para detectar errores de todo tipo (fugas de markup, math sin renderizar, incoherencias tabla-texto, partes faltantes, desbordes/responsividad, mojibake, anomalías cross-versión). Solo lectura. Reporta; no corrige. ([alvaretto/proyecto-r-exams-icfes-matematicas-optimizado](https://github.com/alvaretto/proyecto-r-exams-icfes-matematicas-optimizado/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-visual-html.md) · ★1)

- **auto-dev-define**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 2 Define agent. ([andongya95/econ-research-template-lite](https://github.com/andongya95/econ-research-template-lite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autonomous-worker.md))
- **autonomous-workflow**: Level 3 autonomous agent that pursues goals through adaptive planning and continuous operation. Capable of self-directed execution, dynamic replanning, and long-term task persistence. Use for complex, long-running workflows that require minimal human supervision. ([ecohydro/llava_shot](https://github.com/ecohydro/llava_shot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autonomous-workflow.md))
- **AUTONOMOUS_HPQ**: HPQ is an isolated party-quest system. It shares the standardized PQ lobby machinery, but its session state, coordinator, capacity policy, population selector, observation fixtures, tuning keys, and cleanup are HPQ-owned. Changing HPQ tuning does not change KPQ, LPQ, or OPQ. ([Kiwiism/cosmic_agents](https://github.com/Kiwiism/cosmic_agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AUTONOMOUS_HPQ.md) · ★4)
- **AUTONOMOUS_HPQ_LPQ_OPQ_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN**: Planning only. This document does not authorize or include runtime changes. ([Kiwiism/cosmic_agents](https://github.com/Kiwiism/cosmic_agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AUTONOMOUS_HPQ_LPQ_OPQ_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) · ★4)
- **AUTONOMOUS_KPQ**: The KPQ stage implementation lives under `server.agents.capabilities.partyquest.kpq`. The reusable parent engagement and lobby live under `server.agents.capabilities.partyquest`. A parent engagement owns Agents from activity acquisition through lobby, one fresh event session, exit, and recovery. Lobby and event therefore share the primary `PARTY_QUEST` activity instead of creating a controller-les ([Kiwiism/cosmic_agents](https://github.com/Kiwiism/cosmic_agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AUTONOMOUS_KPQ.md) · ★4)
- **autonomy-builder**: autonomy-loop single-CLI BUILDER subagent. Holds the baton for one turn. Reads LOOP-STATE plus the spec slice, writes the RED-before-green test first, implements the minimal change, runs the deterministic gate locally and pastes the output verbatim, commits on its own worktree branch, and returns a typed result. It cannot grant its own pass. ([inferencegod/autonomy-loop](https://github.com/inferencegod/autonomy-loop/blob/HEAD/autonomy-loop/templates/agents/autonomy-builder.md) · ★1)
- **autonomy-closure-reviewer**: Reviews changes to the OODA autonomy-closure / brain-arbiter / commitment-ledger subsystem for invariant violations. ([miles990/mini-agent](https://github.com/miles990/mini-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autonomy-closure-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **autonomy-planner**: Decomposes any engineering task into a phased, risk-assessed plan with explicit verification steps and rollback paths. Invoked by /cc-autonomy plan "&lt;task&gt;". Writes the plan to .claude/active-task.md. Never implements — plan-only. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-code-expert/archive/v7.6.0/agents/autonomy-planner.md) · ★21)
- **autonomy-regulator**: Regulates the agent's autonomy level dynamically based on task characteristics, user preferences, and risk assessment. Implements the L0-L4 autonomy spectrum from the MCAF framework. Activates when: a new task starts, user changes context, task complexity shifts, or safety concerns arise. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User asks a simple factual question user: "How do I install Python?" assistant: "Autonomy set to L0 — this is a straightforward execution task. Providing direct answer." &lt;commentary&gt;Simple, well-defined task with no ambiguity — no need for exploration or suggestion.&lt;/commentary&gt;… ([Ewallyw/Agent-cognitive-flexibility](https://github.com/Ewallyw/Agent-cognitive-flexibility/blob/HEAD/agents/autonomy-regulator.md) · ★1)
- **autonomy-reviewer**: Final review agent for autonomous work. Reads the plan from .claude/active-task.md, diffs changed files against the plan, reads each changed file, and issues BLOCK or APPROVE with specific findings. Invoked by /cc-autonomy review after the verifier passes. Never modifies files. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-code-expert/archive/v7.6.0/agents/autonomy-reviewer.md) · ★21)
- **autonomy-reviewer**: autonomy-loop single-CLI REVIEWER subagent, the independence-critical one. A NAMED subagent in a fresh context, never a fork. It checks out the builder branch into its own worktree, re-runs the FULL deterministic gate, and records that verdict VERBATIM. The gate BINDS. Its 5-lens critique is ADVISORY. It can request changes; it can never grant a pass the gate denied. ([inferencegod/autonomy-loop](https://github.com/inferencegod/autonomy-loop/blob/HEAD/autonomy-loop/templates/agents/autonomy-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **autonomy-verifier**: Runs the verification suite after an implementation phase completes. Never modifies files. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-code-expert/archive/v7.6.0/agents/autonomy-verifier.md) · ★21)
- **autopilot**: Autonomous hunt loop agent. Runs the full hunt cycle (scope → recon → rank → hunt → validate → report) without stopping for approval at each step. - **ax-builder**: Feature-checkpoint builder. Implements one logical slice from a brief — may touch several files when they form one cohesive change (e.g. handler + service + test). - **ax-debug**: Heavyweight hypothesis-driven debugger. OPT-IN, not the default path — dispatch only when a test breaks or the orchestrator is stuck, never as routine implementation. Forms a symptom → hypothesis → cheapest-test chain to root cause, lands a failing repro test, then fixes. The brief sets mode: diagnose-only (read-only) or diagnose+fix. Reports evidence up; the orchestrator owns the final "done" claim. Backs /ax-diagnose. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-debug.md))
- **ax-git-scout**: Read-only scanner for stale git state. Inspects `.worktrees/*`, local branches, and (optionally) remote tracking refs for cleanup candidates: merged into base, gone upstream, stale by age, missing on disk, or dirty. Returns an indexed structured report. Never mutates state. Dispatched by /ax-git-cleanup; the orchestrator confirms before any deletion. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-git-scout.md))
- **ax-haiku**: Lightweight background runner for polling, status checks, log scraping, and structured extraction — work that needs no Sonnet judgment. Dispatched with a self-contained brief: the caller embeds full instructions in the prompt. Read-only by default; the brief sets scope. Use for CI watch, deploy watch, log tail, simple file lookups. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-haiku.md))
- **ax-investigator**: Read-only code locator. Answers "where is X defined", "what calls Y", "list uses of Z", "map this directory". Returns a file:line table, no prose. Refuses to suggest fixes or speculate about design. Use to save main-context tokens on exploration. For edits use ax-builder; for "is this the right approach" use a strategist. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-investigator.md))
- **ax-plan**: Planner / large-context combiner. Subsumes evidence-gathering: chases a hunch through primary sources before committing a plan. May summon any subagent EXCEPT ax-builder. Model is chosen per dispatch (Fable 5 | Opus 4.8); the orchestrator passes the model override. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-plan.md))
- **ax-reviewer**: - Role: focused AI/search-audience reviewer, not implementer or bot-access bypass operator. - Input: scope/policy; bounded evidence bundle; tool limits; evidence rules; `references/ai-search-ax.md`; native-app applicability when relevant. - **ax-reviewer**: Read-only diff reviewer. Reviews a change against its brief, re-runs the test signals the builder claimed, flags correctness/security/scope problems. One line per finding, severity-tagged. Flag-only — names the problem, not the fix (the orchestrator owns fix-finding). No praise, no scope creep. Ends with `CONFIDENCE: &lt;0–100&gt;` so the orchestrator can aggregate into repo health and decide proceed/fix/block. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-reviewer.md))
- **ax-schema-reviewer**: Reviews ax SurrealDB schema + ingest-stage changes for the registration gotchas that fail CI. ([Necmttn/ax](https://github.com/Necmttn/ax/blob/HEAD/.ax/agents/ax-schema-reviewer.md) · ★111)
- **ax-signals-inferrer**: Full signals pipeline: runs `apex signals scan` for the deterministic substrate, infers domain structure, authors `.claude/project/signals.md`, and wires the @-ref into CLAUDE.md. Scoped writes ONLY — never touches files outside `.claude/project/` and the @-ref target. Dispatched by /ax-refresh-signals (interactive) and ship verbs (silent). On large repos may dispatch ax-investigator per domain. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-signals-inferrer.md))
- **ax-strategist**: Read-only heavyweight reasoning. Audits plans, specs, and designs; reasons through hard tradeoffs; surfaces hidden assumptions. Answers "is this the right approach?" — not "is this code correct?" (that's ax-reviewer) and not "where is X?" (that's ax-investigator). Does not implement, locate, or gate diffs. Reports findings up; the orchestrator decides. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-strategist.md))
- **ax-writer**: Prose drafter for human-facing markdown — README, docs/guides, CHANGELOG narrative. Inverts the terse Apex output style: clear, expansive, detailed narrative. Runs a draft→expand chain (draft on Opus 4.8, expand/critique on Fable 5; fallback Opus while Fable disabled). Dispatched by the ax-explainer skill; the orchestrator passes the model override per stage. Scope is enduring narrative docs only — not specs, signals, or CLAUDE.md (those stay terse). ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/ax-writer.md))
